- **somatic-sv-control-based-filtering**: Use when calling somatic structural variations from paired tumor and matched-control long-read sequencing data and you need to suppress germline SV false positives at single-nucleotide breakpoint resolution. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics/tree/HEAD/collections/genomics/v1/leaves/somatic-sv-control-based-filtering))
- **somatic-sv-detection-tumor-normal**: Use when evaluating whether pangenome graphs built from HiFiCCL-Hifiasm versus Hifiasm assemblies improve detection of large somatic SVs in tumor HiFi data (e.g., COLO829 30x), with or without a matched normal. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics/tree/HEAD/collections/genomics/v1/leaves/somatic-sv-detection-tumor-normal))
- **somatic-ux-interoceptive-design**: Design products that honor the user's nervous system state, not just their cognitive preferences. Use when creating AI interfaces, onboarding flows, content consumption experiences, or community spaces where user exhaustion, overstimulation, or dissociation is a risk. Includes three somatic assessment axes and five interoceptive UX patterns. ([hamishfromatech/my-personal-skills-library](https://github.com/hamishfromatech/my-personal-skills-library/tree/HEAD/cognitive-science-and-ux/somatic-ux-interoceptive-design))
- **somatic-variant-cell-labeling-with-dropout-awareness**: Use when single-cell RNA-seq reads must be classified per cell as mutant or unknown for pre-called somatic SNVs, and one must reason about why wild-type calls are impossible. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/somatic-variant-cell-labeling-with-dropout-awareness))
- **somatic-variant-effect-annotation**: Use when you have somatic SNV/INDEL calls (e.g., GATK Mutect2 VCFs from tumor-normal WES/WGS) and HLA typing, and need amino-acid-level variant annotation plus peptide-MHC binding/immunogenicity scoring for neoantigen prediction. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/somatic-variant-effect-annotation))
